Completion propagation audit: no native whole-programme DONE was found. OMP-280 is REVIEWED with one independent external verification receipt and no native close attempt. Broader historical goals are not complete. MASTER explicitly retains full scope. The smaller completed label is defensible, but its source/fixture “Proven” wording must not migrate into installed/recovery/product-usefulness claims. Existing checkpoint's final active/incomplete sentence conflicts with its own superseding completion paragraphs; propose correction in place during E0.

Some operational labels need reconciliation, not reimplementation: OMP-225 remains BACKLOG although its described CI bootstrap exists, with OMP-210 already DONE. OMP-265 remains BACKLOG despite current functional correction/test proof. Native record changes require their supported route; this audit makes none. No acceptance-criteria shrink was found in the three MASTER common-body comparisons. Later R02 status explicitly adds remaining obligations; current native original233 six criteria and277 five remain intact. This is a bounded comparison, not proof no historical revision ever changed scope.

Representative media journey: actual recurring Plex400 report → source/ledger investigation → OMP-280 six criteria → isolated Gemini implementation → meaningful absence/all-present/create/error/pagination assertions → independent Kimi source review/full isolated suite → exact commits/package/pushed branches. Code reconciles membership before mutation; it does not swallow400. Full NSI compiler was not used. ECC pinned methods were documented, with source fetched after review; no native ECC pack activation established. Lead interrupted optional-memory scope drift, reused worker conversation, provisioned missing venv, investigated baseline fixture drift and integrated/reviewed controls. Owner corrected two process blockers. This is useful reviewed engineering, not unattended fleet or a measured productivity multiplier.

Accounting: Gemini final cumulative reports total753,361 media +3,613,473 controls =4,366,834 tokens; earlier cumulative totals must not be added again. Cache reads3,764,380 +40,917,367 are separately reported; vendor field/pricing semantics must be respected. Native goal1,070,496 tokens/5,892 seconds includes lead activity but not full per-work/model cash attribution. Fable/Kimi/cash/subscription allowance/owner minutes/maintenance unknown. Recorded intervention/repair/reviewer effort is part of cost. No matched capable-agent+ECC+basic-loop baseline exists; 10× remains hypothesis. Keep operating and amortized ownership costs distinct.

## D. Prioritized findings and adequate remedies

### D1. Entire programme was not completed — accepted later scope remains substantive

Classification: **accepted future scope, missing implementation and missing validation**, not retroactive failure of smaller goal. Impact: stopping after a patch/control package abandons full accepted outcome. Evidence: actual goal text, broad paused goals, MASTER complete-system clause,607 explicit inventory rows. Remedy: execute all §E phases, retaining unknown/blocked gates; use shared foundations, not a rewrite. Acceptance: every applicable row satisfies its actual contract or obtains explicit owner-approved disposition; no first-phase completion shortcut.

### D2. Controls target native grant without sufficient pre-action target context

Classification: **confirmed source UX defect**, prior F1 remains. IDs OMP-280 controls/249. `ProgressPanel.tsx` control strip below coordinator card renders live native Pause/Stop; activeWorkKey appears in paused text, StopDialog receives no target. `runControl` resolves `client.execution("")` and fences current WorkService grant; it does not cancel external Codex/Gemini jobs. OMP-280 had no grant. Impact: user may stop different work than inferred from activity card. Remedy: show exact native target before action/confirmation; distinguish external activity and refuse stale/unknown target. Acceptance: two concurrent activity identities, actual named disposable workload affected once; stale target cannot retarget. No new external-job authority.

### D3. Installed controls/recovery remain unqualified; prior “no evidence” must not erase old recovery success

Classification: **missing integrated validation and live authority boundary**, prior F2 valid with narrower wording. IDs249/246/233; preserve277. Real inspect still refuses contract mismatch; wrapper absent. CLI tests use mocked service and daemon tests mock control executable. But old1b78b801 genuinely passed22 installed cases; do not rebuild that foundation. Remedy: integrate/stage compatible candidate via `session-system/runtime/stage.ts` and existing installed tests, isolated PostgreSQL and worker processes. Acceptance: browser→daemon→CLI→service→worker, pause new starts, bounded confirmed termination, committed response loss, restart/reconnect and late cancellation, exact manifest/rollback. Live Q36/cutover separate; unavailable deployment is not failure.

### D4. Controls candidate omits delivered protected-master Web repairs

Classification: **integration gap**, IDs210/225/226/227/249. Remote08a277a contains PR2 CI and daemon/Playwright repairs; controls0a2e0cd is based on earlierd0834bc. Impact: older local failures/weak CI may be rediscovered or shipped despite existing fixes. Remedy: reconcile in isolated integration checkout, preserving all dirty user work and reviewed progress; rerun affected gates on integrated candidate. Acceptance: retained210 changes, intended current fork worker pin, daemon/browser lifecycle checks, exact source identity. No reset of owner checkout; no claim remote CI is web-only.

### D5. Queue/fresh-context proof and checkpoint state remain narrower than lifecycle wording

Classification: **missing validation and record conflict**, prior F3 partially overstated if read as a release-blocking queue mandate. Existing busy event/job/attempt IDs and consumption are real; duplicate suppression proven. Fixture cancellation merely asserts terminal payload, not worker termination; `deadlineAt` is metadata, launcher owns supervision. Predispatch job record was absent because requirement arrived after job completed. Idle/disconnect/restart explicitly unverified and safe wait/event fallback allowed. Remedy: use existing launcher/harness on next real job, store-before-notify, exact current-event consumption, no stale revival, reconcile uncertain send/effects; correct same checkpoint. Acceptance: recover finished/running/uncertain jobs without extra LLM status turns or job rerun. Do not make alpha upgrade/broker/new scheduler a prerequisite.

### D6. Additional recorded defects and incomplete acceptance need evidence-first treatment

Classification: **confirmed OMP-232 defect; other recorded defects/validation gaps**, not blanket assertion all old bugs persist. Tilde helper refusal reproduces; server passes root unchanged, Sidebar lacks visible failure handling. Fix shared path interpretation and UI error, test actual open/failure behavior. OMP-219 queue ordering/skip,248 instruction deadline,262 error-with-write,271 predecessor lifecycle,272 TTSR/abort ordering require current-source qualification before repairs. OMP-265 functional rebase correction already passes real-Git tests; publication/CI/native disposition remain. Full native criteria, including exact Bun1.4 and Fable gates where written, are in §B. Do not inflate sleeps, relax assertions or delete blocker edges to pass.

### D7. Research/learning have working records and primitives, not complete feedback loops

Classification: **missing implementation/validation in accepted programme**, IDsR02–R19/FK/267–270/278–279. Fresh35 research tests prove contracts/custody. They do not execute experiments, protect a scorer, revise hypotheses or improve methods. R04 source remains unchanged with no final independent verdict. Legacy `createLogExperimentTool` accepts agent metric/keep and warns on discrepancy. Knowledge correction changes later compiled context in controlled tests; automatic trace-to-procedure, every-citation verification, actual later task use/outcome and cross-repo counterexample loop remain. Remedy: E4 real experiment-to-reproduction, E5 closed learning, E6 domains/mechanisms, E7 continuous/distributed, E9 recursive downstream confirmation. Acceptance: independent protected evaluation, failures retained, bounded resources, fresh-task confirmation and honest negative outcomes; no narrative/agent score as authority.

### D8. Qualification and economics are not established by available green totals

Classification: **missing validation**, WP5/WP8/R18. No qualifying20-task streak or72-hour mixed run found. Preserve both with exact denominators/strata, zero integrity failures/unplanned workflow repair, baseline/frozen absolute+slowdown limits, all outcomes and usage. Remote Web CI installs unpinned upstream, starts only two browser smoke specs, and some daemon tests return early when stub unavailable; current candidate lacks matching fork qualification. Remedy: fail required qualification visibly on missing/skipped prerequisites; retain optional developer skips without counting them. Capture owner minutes and complete request/cost/maintenance attribution before benefit claims. No new model committee, subscription or synthetic10× threshold.

### D9. Standing upstream watcher is actually failing

Classification: **operational blocker**, OMP-228/230. [Run34845214992](https://github.com/theturtlecsz/oh-my-pi/actions/runs/34845214992) failed September14 at “Create or refresh the tracked review issue”; raw log: repository has disabled issues, exit1. Impact: approved weekly detection does not deliver its durable review through current implementation. Remedy: prepare compatible delivery through existing authorized work/evidence channel, preserving pinned discovery/guardrail and no duplicate effect. Do not enable issues or create/comment on GitHub without required explicit authorization. Acceptance: one traceable grouped stable-upstream review reaches approved record; missing/retry/error paths observable. Critical CI at current main passed separately; watcher failure is not all-CI failure.

### D10. Simplification must retire duplicate responsibilities only after their replacements qualify

Classification: **scoped architectural work and optional cleanup**, existing H/CPK/Fleet/R03/WP owners. Keep WorkService authority, pending-operation journal, immutable evidence, shared context compiler and native acceptance. Combine intake/research/Fleet jobs and resource accounting under existing owner; avoid a research-only scheduler. Reuse actual installer and harness; do not rebuild whole ECC CLI/control plane. Keep one canonical contract generator; no second source/projection truth. Resolve older/newer status copies with evidence links/checkpoint, not another ledger.

CPK0–6 and supervision retirement remain accepted work; delaying broad framework until prerequisites is not canceling it. Each promoted extraction must identify displaced legacy path, equality/negative checks, rollback and net complexity effect. Preserve semantic Advisor coverage and mandatory Observer; only proven classes retire. No worthwhile local simplification is valid. Tradeoffs: retained immutable evidence/storage and explicit trust boundaries cost maintenance but prevent authority loss; universal plugins, extra providers, more reviewers or wholesale rewrites add burden without demonstrated value. Source-specific low findings (dead disabled-account filter, unsupported compatibility KeyError, timeout config) are verification leads, not automatically new product requirements. Confirm actual impact before adding work.

## E. Complete staged execution scope

### E.0 Progress and authority rules

Phases are a dependency graph, not a demand to finish all later foundations before useful release. Complete each row at its stated level. A verified source component can be reused immediately within scope; live activation still requires current qualified dependencies/authority. Earlier phase's blocked live action does not block later independent source work. Conversely, source-development permission does not bypass runtime NSI/Fleet/CPK admission gates. Audit matrix remains frozen; operational progress stays in existing native records, MASTER and the one execution checkpoint.

**E0 — Recover and bind actual state.** Outcome: one implementer can resume without lost work or invented authority. Rows assignedE0 include preserved DONE evidence applicability, continuity, retained-artifact gaps and Q36 preparation. Read exact current records/revisions/owners, remote/candidate identities and jobs; reconcile stale checkpoint status and existing evidence. Preserve original277/243/217/264 and all valid results. Reuse received primary evidence under explicit applicability; no mandatory rerun of all old tests. Resolve missing native ownership before binding affected work; source preparation can proceed under existing external permission. Acceptance: existing checkpoint names actual active goal/scope, candidate, pending effects/jobs, costs, blockers and next ready action. **Proceed immediately to any dependency-ready source work; E0 is not another review-only goal or blanket planning loop.**

**E1 — Dependable ordinary work and intelligible control.** Outcome: owner sees actual work target and can inspect/pause/stop/recover a compatible isolated workload. IDs249/246/233, preserve277; relevant203/214/219/225–227/232/248/262/263/265/271/272; CONTROL/QUEUE/LIFE/SQL rows. Start by integrating current protected heads with reviewed candidates and preserving dirty user work. Verify old alleged failures before edits. Correct confirmed target/tilde defects. Reuse staging, typed controls, pending operations, installed-process tests and safe fallback. Validate candidate isolation, submissions/late writes, stale grants, operation replay, stop vs pause, worker termination, UI reconnect, runtime/DB/WAL compatibility/restore. Native queue219 must qualify before multi-item use; initial single-item path may ship first. Acceptance: exact compatible immutable candidate, raw process/service/browser evidence, required gates and independent review; correct refusal of incompatible/missing authority. Package concrete install/rollback proposal; authorize live cutover separately. **Proceed toE2/E3 independent work after relevant source/interface proof, retaining live blocker.**

**E2 — Substantial ECC and economical native engineering.** Outcome: relevant upstream methods actually activate through owned native packs, reliable audit and accountable economical routes. WP1–6 plus prerequisiteWP8/R00/R01/COST1–3; existing model/routing/audit/accounting owners. 4. **Upstream-watch delivery:** issues are disabled and repository prohibits automatic issue creation. Recommend existing authorized durable work/evidence channel. If changing channel/credentials needs owner policy, prepare exact change and ask once at final boundary; no enable-issues workaround.
5. **Optional products/exposure/hardware:** no decision needed to start independent scope. Keep conditional re-entry visible; do not invent authorization or defer an already accepted mandatory backend by preference.

No additional product-preference answer is required to start E0/E1 source work. A phase's documentation or owner proposal is not implementation, acceptance or an approved scope disposition.
